*Ewan Cameron*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个双轨管道检测文学小说中的圣经典故，并应用于科马克·麦卡锡的小说集。管道结合自下而上的嵌入追踪与自上而下的语域追踪，通过级联LLM审查，在语料库中发现349处典故，在115个先前记录的典故中独立恢复了62个（54%召回率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文学作品中圣经典故的传统研究依赖细读（close reading），效率低且难以扩展到大规模语料库；同时，基于词频的自动化方法易遗漏依赖语域或转化的典故形式。研究旨在借助LLM提升典故检测的召回率与可扩展性。

Method: 双轨管道：（1）自下而上嵌入轨——利用逆文档频率（IDTF）识别与KJV共享的稀有词汇，在局部上下文中嵌入候选段落，通过级联LLM审查；（2）自上而下语域轨——让LLM无定向地阅读麦卡锡散文以发现语域碰撞。两种追踪结果由长上下文模型跨文本验证（将整部小说与KJV同时纳入单一上下文窗口），所有发现均与已发表学术文献交叉验证。

Result: 管道在语料库中识别出349处典故；在115个先前记录的典故中独立恢复62个（54%召回率）；召回率因典故类型而异——转化意象30%，语域碰撞80%。

Conclusion: LLM作为机械文体分析辅助工具具有显著价值；该方法可促进大规模文学语料库中互文性的统计分析研究；同时揭示了不同典故类型的检测难度差异，语域碰撞最易检测，转化意象最难。

Abstract: We present a dual-track pipeline for detecting biblical allusions in literary fiction and apply it to the novels of Cormac McCarthy. A bottom-up embedding track uses inverse document frequency to identify rare vocabulary shared with the King James Bible, embeds occurrences in their local context for sense disambiguation, and passes candidate passage pairs through cascaded LLM review. A top-down register track asks an LLM to read McCarthy's prose undirected to any specific biblical passage for comparison, catching allusions not distinguished by word or phrase rarity. Both tracks are cross-validated by a long-context model that holds entire novels alongside the KJV in a single pass, and every finding is checked against published scholarship. Restricting attention to allusions that carry a textual echo--shared phrasing, reworked vocabulary, or transplanted cadence--and distinguishing literary allusions proper from signposted biblical references (similes naming biblical figures, characters overtly citing scripture), the pipeline surfaces 349 allusions across the corpus. Among a target set of 115 previously documented allusions retrieved through human review of the academic literature, the pipeline independently recovers 62 (54% recall), with recall varying by connection type from 30% (transformed imagery) to 80% (register collisions). We contextualise these results with respect to the value-add from LLMs as assistants to mechanical stylometric analyses, and their potential to facilitate the statistical study of intertextuality in massive literary corpora.

</details>


### [58] [LePREC: Reasoning as Classification over Structured Factors for Assessing Relevance of Legal Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19464)
*Fanyu Wang,Xiaoxi Kang,Paul Burgess,Aashish Srivastava,Chetan Arora,Adnan Trakic,Lay-Ki Soon,Md Khalid Hossain,Lizhen Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型在法律议题识别任务中的能力进行评估，发现GPT-4o精确率仅62%，为此提出LePREC框架，通过神经-符号结合的方法实现了30-40%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超过一半的人口面临法律资源匮乏的问题，而LLM在法律议题识别这一基础步骤上仍存在显著局限——GPT-4o精确率仅62%，表明现有模型难以准确识别法律争议焦点。

Method: (1) 构建数据集：从769个马来西亚合同法真实案例中提取事实，生成候选法律议题，由资深法律专家标注；(2) 提出LePREC框架：神经组件利用LLM将法律描述转化为问答对表示多样化分析因素，符号组件对离散特征应用稀疏线性模型学习显式代数权重以识别关键推理因素。

Result: LePREC在法律议题识别任务上相比GPT-4o、Claude等先进LLM基线模型实现了30-40%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于相关性分析的因素-议题分析方法为法律相关性判断提供了更数据高效解决方案；神经-符号融合架构在保持可解释性（透明特征权重）的同时实现了数据效率（相关性统计分类）。

Abstract: More than half of the global population struggles to meet their civil justice needs due to limited legal resources. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive reasoning capabilities, significant challenges remain even at the foundational step of legal issue identification. To investigate LLMs' capabilities in this task, we constructed a dataset from 769 real-world Malaysian Contract Act court cases, using GPT-4o to extract facts and generate candidate legal issues, annotated by senior legal experts, which reveals a critical limitation: while LLMs generate diverse issue candidates, their precision remains inadequate (GPT-4o achieves only 62%). To address this gap, we propose LePREC (Legal Professional-inspired Reasoning Elicitation and Classification), a neuro-symbolic framework combining neural generation with structured statistical reasoning. LePREC consists of: (1) a neuro component leverages LLMs to transform legal descriptions into question-answer pairs representing diverse analytical factors, and (2) a symbolic component applies sparse linear models over these discrete features, learning explicit algebraic weights that identify the most informative reasoning factors. Unlike end-to-end neural approaches, LePREC achieves interpretability through transparent feature weighting while maintaining data efficiency through correlation-based statistical classification. Experiments show a 30-40% improvement over advanced LLM baselines, including GPT-4o and Claude, confirming that correlation-based factor-issue analysis offers a more data-efficient solution for relevance decisions.

</details>


### [59] [Rank-Turbulence Delta and Interpretable Approaches to Stylometric Delta Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19499)
*Dmitry Pronin,Evgeny Kazartsev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新的作者归属度量方法——Rank-Turbulence Delta和Jensen-Shannon Delta，通过应用针对概率分布设计的距离函数来推广Burrows经典Delta方法。通过将词频向量重新解释为概率分布，并开发token级别的分解实现数值可解释性。在英语、德语、法语和俄语四种文学语料库上进行评估，Rank-Turbulence Delta达到与Cosine Delta相当的归属准确率，Jensen-Shannon Delta则一致性地匹配或超越Burrows Delta的表现在扩展的SOCIOLIT语料库（包含180位作者的755部作品）上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Burrows经典Delta方法存在局限性，需要开发更具理论基础的度量方法；同时，现有方法缺乏数值可解释性，难以支持精细化阅读和结果验证。

Method: 1) 将词频向量重新解释为概率分布；2) 对比中心化与非中心化z-score方法；3) 开发token级别的Delta距离分解实现数值可解释性；4) 在四种语言语料库上验证

Result: Rank-Turbulence Delta准确率与Cosine Delta相当；Jensen-Shannon Delta一致性地匹配或超越Burrows Delta；在包含755部作品、180位作者的SOCIOLIT俄语语料库上验证有效

Conclusion: 两种新方法有效推广了经典Delta度量，通过概率分布框架和数值可解释性设计，为作者归属分析提供了更 robust 的工具

Abstract: This article introduces two new measures for authorship attribution - Rank-Turbulence Delta and Jensen-Shannon Delta - which generalise Burrows's classical Delta by applying distance functions designed for probabilistic distributions. We first set out the theoretical basis of the measures, contrasting centred and uncentred z-scoring of word-frequency vectors and re-casting the uncentred vectors as probability distributions. Building on this representation, we develop a token-level decomposition that renders every Delta distance numerically interpretable, thereby facilitating close reading and the validation of results. The effectiveness of the methods is assessed on four literary corpora in English, German, French and Russian. The English, German and French datasets are compiled from Project Gutenberg, whereas the Russian benchmark is the SOCIOLIT corpus containing 755 works by 180 authors spanning the eighteenth to the twenty-first centuries. Rank-Turbulence Delta attains attribution accuracy comparable with Cosine Delta; Jensen-Shannon Delta consistently matches or exceeds the performance of canonical Burrows's Delta. Finally, several established attribution algorithms are re-evaluated on the extended SOCIOLIT corpus.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Rating: A Comprehensive Evaluation and Benchmark for AI Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19502)
*Bowen Li,Haochen Ma,Yuxin Wang,Jie Yang,Xinchi Chen,Xuanjing Huang,Yining Zheng,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个名为Beyond Rating的AI审稿评估框架，超越传统评分预测任务，从内容忠实性、论证一致性、焦点一致性、问题建设性和AI可能性五个维度评估AI审稿人。核心贡献是提出基于文本的评估指标，特别是弱点论证召回率能与人类偏好高度相关，为自动审稿研究提供可靠标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM自动审稿的基准测试仅关注评分预测任务，忽视了审稿的核心价值在于文本论证本身。研究者认为审稿的实用性体现在其文本论证、问题提出和批评内容，而非简单的标量分数。

Method: 1) 提出五维评估框架（Content Faithfulness、Argumentative Alignment、Focus Consistency、Question Constructiveness、AI-Likelihood）；2) 引入Max-Recall策略以容纳有效的专家分歧；3) 构建经过严格过滤的高置信度论文审稿数据集，去除程序性噪声。

Result: 传统n-gram指标无法反映人类偏好，而本文提出的文本中心指标，特别是弱点论证的召回率，与评分准确性高度相关。

Conclusion: AI批评焦点与人类专家保持一致是实现可靠自动评分的前提条件，该框架为未来研究提供了稳健的评估标准。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) has spurred interest in automated peer review; however, progress is currently stifled by benchmarks that treat reviewing primarily as a rating prediction task. We argue that the utility of a review lies in its textual justification--its arguments, questions, and critique--rather than a scalar score. To address this, we introduce Beyond Rating, a holistic evaluation framework that assesses AI reviewers across five dimensions: Content Faithfulness, Argumentative Alignment, Focus Consistency, Question Constructiveness, and AI-Likelihood. Notably, we propose a Max-Recall strategy to accommodate valid expert disagreement and introduce a curated dataset of paper with high-confidence reviews, rigorously filtered to remove procedural noise. Extensive experiments demonstrate that while traditional n-gram metrics fail to reflect human preferences, our proposed text-centric metrics--particularly the recall of weakness arguments--correlate strongly with rating accuracy. These findings establish that aligning AI critique focus with human experts is a prerequisite for reliable automated scoring, offering a robust standard for future research.

</details>


### [61] [Bangla Key2Text: Text Generation from Keywords for a Low Resource Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19508)
*Tonmoy Talukder,G M Shahariar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Bangla Key2Text，一个包含260万对孟加拉语关键词-文本对的大规模数据集，用于低资源语言的关键词驱动文本生成任务。基于BERT的关键词抽取流水线构建数据集，并微调mT5和BanglaT5模型进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语作为低资源语言，缺乏大规模的关键词驱动文本生成数据集和研究，现有大型语言模型在零样本条件下的生成效果有待提升。

Method: 采用基于BERT的关键词抽取流水线从数百万孟加拉语新闻文本中提取关键词，构建260万对关键词-文本对数据集；在此数据集上微调mT5和BanglaT5两种序列到序列模型。

Result: 任务特定的微调模型在孟加拉语关键词条件文本生成任务上显著优于零样本大型语言模型；公开了数据集、训练模型和代码。

Conclusion: Bangla Key2Text为孟加拉语自然语言生成和关键词到文本生成任务提供了新的基准资源；实验证明了有监督微调在该任务上的有效性。

Abstract: This paper introduces \textit{Bangla Key2Text}, a large-scale dataset of $2.6$ million Bangla keyword--text pairs designed for keyword-driven text generation in a low-resource language. The dataset is constructed using a BERT-based keyword extraction pipeline applied to millions of Bangla news texts, transforming raw articles into structured keyword--text pairs suitable for supervised learning. To establish baseline performance on this new benchmark, we fine-tune two sequence-to-sequence models, \texttt{mT5} and \texttt{BanglaT5}, and evaluate them using multiple automatic metrics and human judgments. Experimental results show that task-specific fine-tuning substantially improves keyword-conditioned text generation in Bangla compared to zero-shot large language models. The dataset, trained models, and code are publicly released to support future research in Bangla natural language generation and keyword-to-text generation tasks.

</details>


### [62] [Emotion-Cause Pair Extraction in Conversations via Semantic Decoupling and Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19547)
*Tianxiang Ma,Weijie Feng,Xinyu Wang,Zhiyong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对对话中的情感-原因对抽取（ECPEC）任务，提出通过语义解耦将情绪和原因语义映射到互补表示空间，并利用最优传输实现全局对齐的SCALE框架，有效解决了现有方法在多对多因果关系捕捉上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将ECPEC建模为独立的成对分类任务，存在两个主要局限：1）忽略了情绪扩散和原因解释的语义差异；2）无法捕捉全局一致的多对多对话因果关系。

Method: 提出了SCALE统一框架，包含三个核心步骤：1）语义解耦：将对话中的情绪导向语义和原因导向语义分离，映射到两个互补的表示空间；2）全局对齐：将ECPEC重新定义为情绪侧和原因侧表示之间的全局对齐问题；3）最优传输：利用最优传输理论实现多对多且全局一致的情感-原因匹配。整个框架在共享对话结构内实例化语义解耦和对齐原则。

Result: 实验结果表明，SCALE在多个基准数据集上始终达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 提出的SCALE框架在多个基准数据集上取得了最先进的性能，代码已开源。该方法通过语义解耦和全局对齐的思路，有效解决了传统方法在捕捉多对多因果关系和全局一致性方面的不足。

Abstract: Emotion-Cause Pair Extraction in Conversations (ECPEC) aims to identify the set of causal relations between emotion utterances and their triggering causes within a dialogue. Most existing approaches formulate ECPEC as an independent pairwise classification task, overlooking the distinct semantics of emotion diffusion and cause explanation, and failing to capture globally consistent many-to-many conversational causality. To address these limitations, we revisit ECPEC from a semantic perspective and seek to disentangle emotion-oriented semantics from cause-oriented semantics, mapping them into two complementary representation spaces to better capture their distinct conversational roles. Building on this semantic decoupling, we naturally formulate ECPEC as a global alignment problem between the emotion-side and cause-side representations, and employ optimal transport to enable many-to-many and globally consistent emotion-cause matching. Based on this perspective, we propose a unified framework SCALE that instantiates the above semantic decoupling and alignment principle within a shared conversational structure. Extensive experiments on several benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCALE consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our codes are released at https://github.com/CoCoSphere/SCALE.

</details>


### [63] [Detecting Hallucinations in SpeechLLMs at Inference Time Using Attention Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19565)
*Jonas Waldendorf,Bashar Awwad Shiekh Hasan,Evgenii Tsymbalov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出利用注意力模式检测 SpeechLLM 中的幻觉，通过四个音频相关指标和轻量级分类器实现高效检测，在多模型多任务上取得显著提升，并具有良好的域外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SpeechLLM 中的幻觉会带来重大风险，但现有检测方法依赖成本高昂或难以获取的金标准输出，且文本 LLM 的检测方法无法直接捕捉音频特异性信号。

Method: 使用四个注意力衍生指标（AUDIORATIO、AUDIOCONSISTENCY、AUDIOENTROPY、TEXTENTROPY）捕捉与幻觉相关的病态注意力模式，并训练轻量级逻辑回归分类器进行推理时检测。

Result: 在 ASR 和语音到文本翻译任务上，该方法在域内数据上优于基于不确定性和先前注意力的基线，提升高达 +0.23 PR-AUC，并能泛化到域外 ASR 设置；约 100 个注意力头即可获得良好性能。

Conclusion: 注意力模式为 SpeechLLM 中的幻觉检测提供了有价值的工具。虽然有效性依赖于模型，且需要针对特定任务进行训练，但该方法在域内和域外场景均展现出良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Hallucinations in Speech Large Language Models (SpeechLLMs) pose significant risks, yet existing detection methods typically rely on gold-standard outputs that are costly or impractical to obtain. Moreover, hallucination detection methods developed for text-based LLMs do not directly capture audio-specific signals. We investigate four attention-derived metrics: AUDIORATIO, AUDIOCONSISTENCY, AUDIOENTROPY, and TEXTENTROPY, designed to capture pathological attention patterns associated with hallucination, and train lightweight logistic regression classifiers on these features for efficient inference-time detection. Across automatic speech recognition and speech-to-text translation tasks, evaluations on Qwen-2-Audio and Voxtral-3B show that our approach outperforms uncertainty-based and prior attention-based baselines on in-domain data, achieving improvements of up to +0.23 PR-AUC, and generalises to out-of-domain ASR settings. We further find that strong performance can be achieved with approximately 100 attention heads, improving out-of-domain generalisation compared to using all heads. While effectiveness is model-dependent and task-specific training is required, our results demonstrate that attention patterns provide a valuable tool for hallucination detection in SpeechLLMs.

</details>


### [64] [A Self-Evolving Framework for Efficient Terminal Agents via Observational Context Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19572)
*Jincheng Ren,Siwei Wu,Yizhi Li,Kang Zhu,Shu Xu,Boyu Feng,Ruibin Yuan,Wei Zhang,Riza Batista-Navarro,Jian Yang,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TACO是一种即插即用的自进化终端智能体压缩框架，通过从交互轨迹中自动发现和提炼压缩规则来解决长时域、多轮终端任务中的令牌成本二次增长问题，在多个基准测试上实现了1%-4%的性能提升，同时减少约10%的令牌开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型能力提升，研究转向长时域、多轮终端任务，但保留原始环境反馈导致严重冗余，令牌成本随步数二次增长。现有观测压缩方法（启发式或固定提示词）因终端环境异构性难以泛化。

Method: 提出TACO框架，一种即插即用、自进化的终端智能体压缩方案。核心机制：从交互轨迹中自动发现并持续优化压缩规则，无需人工设计，针对现有终端智能体实现自适应压缩。

Result: 在TerminalBench（TB 1.0和TB 2.0）及四个额外基准（SWE-Bench Lite、CompileBench、DevEval、CRUST-Bench）上验证。跨主流智能体框架和骨干模型均有效：MiniMax-2.5下性能提升且令牌开销降低~10%；TerminalBench上强智能体模型一致提升1%-4%；相同令牌预算下精度再增2%-3%。

Conclusion: 自进化、任务感知的压缩方法对终端智能体有效且具有良好泛化性。

Abstract: As model capabilities advance, research has increasingly shifted toward long-horizon, multi-turn terminal-centric agentic tasks, where raw environment feedback is often preserved in the interaction history to support future decisions. However, repeatedly retaining such feedback introduces substantial redundancy and causes cumulative token cost to grow quadratically with the number of steps, hindering long-horizon reasoning. Although observation compression can mitigate this issue, the heterogeneity of terminal environments makes heuristic-based or fixed-prompt methods difficult to generalize. We propose TACO, a plug-and-play, self-evolving Terminal Agent Compression framework that automatically discovers and refines compression rules from interaction trajectories for existing terminal agents. Experiments on TerminalBench (TB 1.0 and TB 2.0) and four additional terminal-related benchmarks (i.e., SWE-Bench Lite, CompileBench, DevEval, and CRUST-Bench) show that TACO consistently improves performance across mainstream agent frameworks and strong backbone models. With MiniMax-2.5, it improves performance on most benchmarks while reducing token overhead by around 10%. On TerminalBench, it brings consistent gains of 1%-4% across strong agentic models, and further improves accuracy by around 2%-3% under the same token budget. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and generalization of self-evolving, task-aware compression for terminal agents.

</details>


### [65] [A Bolu: A Structured Dataset for the Computational Analysis of Sardinian Improvisational Poetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19584)
*Silvio Calderaro,Johanna Monti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: A Bolu是首个针对萨丁尼亚语cantada logudorese变体的即兴诗歌结构化语料库，包含2,835节共141,321个词符。研究结合描述性统计与计算语言学技术进行多维分析，验证了Parry-Lord公式化理论在即兴诗歌中的应用，为少数民族语言的NLP发展提供重要资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言处理在少数民族语言领域的研究尚未有效保护口语语言遗产，尤其是基于实时即兴创作的押韵诗歌这一表演性体裁在计算语言学中几乎未被探索。需要创建特定资源来记录和分析即兴诗歌的结构特征。

Method: 构建A Bolu语料库并应用多维分析方法：(1)结合描述性统计指标；(2)运用计算语言学技术；(3)对诗歌文本特征进行系统映射分析。

Result: 萨丁尼亚语即兴诗人的创作呈现高度重复性模式，这一发现支持了Parry和Lord的公式化理论，表明口头即兴创作依赖于预制的语言模块和程式化表达。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解口头创作机制提供了新视角，同时为开发更具包容性且能响应小众语言特殊需求的NLP工具做出了重要贡献，对少数民族语言遗产保护具有重要意义。

Abstract: The growing interest of Natural Language Processing (NLP) in minority languages has not yet bridged the gap in the preservation of oral linguistic heritage. In particular, extemporaneous poetry - a performative genre based on real-time improvisation, metrical-rhetorical competence - remains a largely unexplored area of computational linguistics. This methodological gap necessitates the creation of specific resources to document and analyse the structures of improvised poetry. This is the context in which A Bolu was created, the first structured corpus of extemporaneous poetry dedicated to cantada logudorese, a variant of the Sardinian language. The dataset comprises 2,835 stanzas for a total of 141,321 tokens. The study presents the architecture of the corpus and applies a multidimensional analysis combining descriptive statistical indices and computational linguistics techniques to map the characteristics of the poetic text. The results indicate that the production of Sardinian extemporaneous poets is characterised by recurring patterns that support Parry and Lord's theory of formulaicity. This evidence not only provides a new key to understanding oral creativity, but also offers a significant contribution to the development of NLP tools that are more inclusive and sensitive to the specificities of less widely spoken languages.

</details>


### [66] [Cross-Model Consistency of AI-Generated Exercise Prescriptions: A Repeated Generation Study Across Three Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19598)
*Kihyuk Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究在temperature=0条件下比较GPT-4.1、Claude Sonnet 4.6和Gemini 2.5 Flash三款大语言模型生成运动处方的一致性。通过6个临床场景各生成20次处方（共360个输出），从语义相似度、输出可重复性、FITT分类和安全性四个维度评估。GPT-4.1语义相似度最高（0.955）且100%输出唯一；Gemini 2.5 Flash相似度高（0.950）但源于文本重复而非一致推理；Claude Sonnet 4.6相似度最低（0.903）。结果表明模型选择是临床决策而非单纯技术决策，重复生成条件下的输出行为应作为LLM运动处方系统部署的核心评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动处方的临床应用对输出一致性和可靠性有严格要求，但现有研究多基于单次生成评估，无法揭示模型在重复条件下的真实行为差异。该研究旨在系统比较三款主流LLM在确定性设置下的生成一致性，为临床运动处方系统的模型选择提供实证依据。

Method: 实验设置：temperature=0（确定性生成），6个临床场景，每个场景每个模型生成20次，共360个输出。评估维度：语义相似度（采用文本嵌入相似性度量）、输出可重现性（计算唯一输出占比）、FITT分类一致性（Frequency, Intensity, Time, Type）、安全性表达。采用Kruskal-Wallis H检验进行模型间差异显著性分析。

Result: 语义相似度：GPT-4.1（0.955）> Gemini 2.5 Flash（0.950）> Claude Sonnet 4.6（0.903），组间差异显著（H=458.41, p<.001）。关键发现：GPT-4.1生成100%唯一输出且语义稳定；Gemini 2.5 Flash仅27.5%唯一输出，高相似度源于文本重复而非一致的推理逻辑。安全性表达在所有模型均达到上限，无法作为区分指标。

Conclusion: 在temperature=0的确定性设置下，三款模型表现出本质不同的生成行为模式，仅凭语义相似度无法区分"稳定推理"与"文本重复"。模型选择应视为临床决策，需综合考虑输出唯一性和推理一致性。重复生成条件下的行为表现应成为LLM临床部署的核心评估标准，单次输出评估存在重大局限。

Abstract: This study compared repeated generation consistency of exercise prescription outputs across three large language models (LLMs), specifically GPT-4.1, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Gemini 2.5 Flash, under temperature=0 conditions. Each model generated prescriptions for six clinical scenarios 20 times, yielding 360 total outputs analyzed across four dimensions: semantic similarity, output reproducibility, FITT classification, and safety expression. Mean semantic similarity was highest for GPT-4.1 (0.955), followed by Gemini 2.5 Flash (0.950) and Claude Sonnet 4.6 (0.903), with significant inter-model differences confirmed (H = 458.41, p < .001). Critically, these scores reflected fundamentally different generative behaviors: GPT-4.1 produced entirely unique outputs (100%) with stable semantic content, while Gemini 2.5 Flash showed pronounced output repetition (27.5% unique outputs), indicating that its high similarity score derived from text duplication rather than consistent reasoning. Identical decoding settings thus yielded fundamentally different consistency profiles, a distinction that single-output evaluations cannot capture. Safety expression reached ceiling levels across all models, confirming its limited utility as a differentiating metric. These results indicate that model selection constitutes a clinical rather than merely technical decision, and that output behavior under repeated generation conditions should be treated as a core criterion for reliable deployment of LLM-based exercise prescription systems.

</details>


### [67] [The "Small World of Words" German Free-Association Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19620)
*Samuel Aeschbach,Rui Mata,Kaidi Lõo,Simon De Deyne,Dirk U. Wulff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SWOW-DE是迄今为止最大的德语自由联想数据库，包含5,877个德语词汇的联想数据，作为多语言Small World of Words项目的一部分发布。该数据集可有效预测词汇决策任务、语义相关判断和心理语言学词汇评分中的表现，并可与其他德语资源及跨语言数据进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 英语、荷兰语、西班牙语和汉语等语言已有大规模自由联想常模，但德语缺乏此类资源，限制了德语认知语言学、语义学和跨文化研究的开展。

Method: 研究团队收集了德语词汇的自由联想数据，描述了数据收集程序、被试特征及完整的预处理流程，并使用词汇决策任务、语义相关判断和心理语言学评分三种范式验证数据质量。

Result: SWOW-DE数据能够稳健预测被试在多种心理语言学任务中的表现，与现有德语资源对比表现良好，跨语言比较揭示了共享与语言特异性的联想模式。

Conclusion: SWOW-DE是迄今最大的德语自由联想数据库，可作为语言学、心理学和跨文化研究的独特资源，并指明了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Free-association norms provide essential empirical data for investigating linguistic, semantic, and cultural phenomena in the cognitive sciences. Although large-scale norms exist for languages such as English, Dutch, Spanish, and Mandarin Chinese, no comparable resource has been available for German. To address this gap, we present free-association norms for 5,877 German cue words as part of the German version of the multilingual Small World of Words (SWOW) project. We describe the data collection procedures, participant characteristics, and our comprehensive preprocessing pipeline before introducing the resulting SWOW-DE data set. Using data from three established psycholinguistic paradigms, we show that SWOW-DE norms robustly predict performance in lexical decision tasks, relatedness judgments, and psycholinguistic word ratings. Furthermore, we demonstrate that SWOW-DE responses compare favorably with existing German resources and provide a preliminary cross-linguistic comparison revealing both shared and language-specific association patterns, highlighting promising directions for future research. Overall, SWOW-DE represents the largest collection of German free associations to date and offers a unique resource for linguistic, psychological, and cross-cultural research.

</details>


### [68] [Micro Language Models Enable Instant Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19642)
*Wen Cheng,Tuochao Chen,Karim Helwani,Sriram Srinivasan,Luke Zettlemoyer,Shyamnath Gollakota*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出微型语言模型(μLMs，8M-30M参数)，在边缘设备上即时生成回复的前4-8个词以掩盖云端延迟，云端模型随后完成剩余内容。通过协作生成框架实现无缝的句中切换和错误恢复，实验表明这种极不对称的协作模式是可行的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备(如智能手表、智能眼镜)受限于功耗和算力，无法运行最小的100M-1B参数语言模型；而云端推理存在多秒级延迟，破坏了智能助手的响应体验感。

Method: 设计了微型语言模型μLMs(8M-30M参数)，在设备端即时生成上下文相关回复的前4-8个词；同时重新定义云端模型为"续写者"而非"应答者"，实现句中无缝交接；并设计了三种错误纠正方法实现结构化的优雅恢复。

Result: 极小规模的语言生成仍具有实用性，μLMs模型性能可匹配甚至超越现有的70M-256M参数规模模型；实现了设备端启动响应、云端无缝完成的协作框架。

Conclusion: 证明了数量级不对称协作(micro-to-large collaboration)的可行性，为资源极度受限设备上的实时AI应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Edge devices such as smartwatches and smart glasses cannot continuously run even the smallest 100M-1B parameter language models due to power and compute constraints, yet cloud inference introduces multi-second latencies that break the illusion of a responsive assistant. We introduce micro language models ($μ$LMs): ultra-compact models (8M-30M parameters) that instantly generate the first 4-8 words of a contextually grounded response on-device, while a cloud model completes it; thus, masking the cloud latency. We show that useful language generation survives at this extreme scale with our models matching several 70M-256M-class existing models. We design a collaborative generation framework that reframes the cloud model as a continuator rather than a respondent, achieving seamless mid-sentence handoffs and structured graceful recovery via three error correction methods when the local opener goes wrong. Empirical results show that $μ$LMs can initiate responses that larger models complete seamlessly, demonstrating that orders-of-magnitude asymmetric collaboration is achievable and unlocking responsive AI for extremely resource-constrained devices. The model checkpoint and demo are available at https://github.com/Sensente/micro_language_model_swen_project.

</details>


### [69] [The signal is the ceiling: Measurement limits of LLM-predicted experience ratings from open-ended survey text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19645)
*Andrew Hong,Jason Potteiger,Luis E. Zapata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper demonstrates that prompt design and model selection have limited impact on LLM accuracy for predicting fan experience ratings — together explaining far less variance than the linguistic content of the input text itself. The research identifies a two-part performance ceiling: one accessible to prompt engineering (model reading bias) and one not (information gap between written text and actual decisions). The takeaway is that prompt engineering helps in specific, predictable ways rather than generally improving performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: An earlier study demonstrated that an unoptimized GPT 4.1 prompt achieved 67% within +/-1 agreement in predicting fan-reported experience ratings. This raised an empirical question: what is the relative contribution of prompt design versus model selection to that performance? Understanding these levers is critical for resource allocation in LLM deployment for sentiment/feedback analysis.

Method: The study employed a crossed factorial design comparing two prompt conditions (original baseline vs. moderately customized) across three GPT models (4.1, 4.1-mini, 5.2) on approximately 10,000 post-game surveys from five MLB teams. Accuracy was measured using within +/-1 point agreement between model predictions and fan-reported experience ratings.

Result: Prompt customization yielded a modest ~2 percentage point improvement (67% → 69%), while model upgrades degraded or maintained baseline performance. Critically, accuracy variation across capable configurations was over an order of magnitude greater attributable to linguistic character of input text than to prompt or model choices. The performance ceiling has two components: (1) model reading bias addressable by prompt design, and (2) the gap between what fans write about versus what they actually decide — the latter being fundamentally unresolvable through engineering since the missing information is absent from the text.

Conclusion: The paper reveals that prompt engineering's value is "specific and predictable" — it only helps with biases in how models read text, not with the fundamental gap between written content and actual fan decisions. Model selection showed no reliable improvement, suggesting that once a capable model is chosen, further model upgrades yield diminishing returns. The research reframes prompt engineering from a "helps a little" narrative to a targeted intervention tool with clear boundaries on its effective scope.

Abstract: An earlier paper (Hong, Potteiger, and Zapata 2026) established that an unoptimized GPT 4.1 prompt predicts fan-reported experience ratings within one point 67% of the time from open-ended survey text. This paper tests the relative impact of prompt design and model selection on that performance. We compared four configurations on approximately 10,000 post-game surveys from five MLB teams: the original baseline prompt and a moderately customized version, crossed with three GPT models (4.1, 4.1-mini, 5.2). Prompt customization added roughly two percentage points of within +/-1 agreement on GPT 4.1 (from 67% to 69%). Both model swaps from that best configuration degraded performance: GPT 5.2 returned to the baseline, and GPT 4.1-mini fell six percentage points below it. Both levers combined were dwarfed by the input itself: across capable configurations, accuracy varied more than an order of magnitude more by the linguistic character of the text than by the choice of prompt or model. The ceiling has two parts. One is a bias in how the model reads text, which prompt design can correct. The other is a difference between what fans write about and what they actually decide, which no engineering can close because the missing information is not in the text. Prompt customization moved the first part; model selection moved neither reliably. The result is not that "prompt engineering helps a little" but that prompt engineering helps in a specific and predictable way, on the part of the ceiling it can reach.

</details>


### [70] [Exploring Language-Agnosticity in Function Vectors: A Case Study in Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19678)
*Nurkhan Laiyk,Gerard I. Gállego,Javier Ferrando,Fajri Koto*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探索了函数向量（FVs）是否具有语言无关性。研究发现翻译函数向量能够跨语言迁移，并在不同模型变体和粒度级别间保持有效性，证明了任务向量表示的跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已表明多语言模型表示具有语言无关性，但函数向量是否具有同样的特性尚不清楚。本研究旨在探索函数向量是否表现出语言无关性，以扩展对任务级表示迁移性的理解。

Method: 使用机器翻译作为案例研究，在三个解码器-only的多语言LLM中进行实验。通过提取单一英语→目标语言的翻译函数向量，观察其向其他未见语言迁移的效果。实验还包括消融实验以验证函数向量的作用，并对比基座模型与指令微调变体间的迁移能力，以及词级到句级翻译的泛化程度。

Result: 翻译函数向量能够从单一英语→目标方向迁移到其他目标语言，持续提升多种未见语言中正确翻译令牌的排名。消融实验表明移除函数向量会降低跨语言翻译性能，对非相关任务影响有限。基座模型的函数向量可迁移至指令微调变体，并部分实现从词级到句级翻译的泛化。

Conclusion: 函数向量展现出跨语言的泛化能力，能够从一种源语言方向迁移到其他目标语言，并在指令微调模型和从词级到句级的翻译任务中保持有效性。这一发现扩展了现有研究，证明任务向量表示的语言无关性同样适用于函数向量层面。

Abstract: Function vectors (FVs) are vector representations of tasks extracted from model activations during in-context learning. While prior work has shown that multilingual model representations can be language-agnostic, it remains unclear whether the same holds for function vectors. We study whether FVs exhibit language-agnosticity, using machine translation as a case study. Across three decoder-only multilingual LLMs, we find that translation FVs extracted from a single English$\rightarrow$Target direction transfer to other target languages, consistently improving the rank of correct translation tokens across multiple unseen languages. Ablation results show that removing the FV degrades translation across languages with limited impact on unrelated tasks. We further show that base-model FVs transfer to instruction-tuned variants and partially generalize from word-level to sentence-level translation.

</details>


### [71] [An Answer is just the Start: Related Insight Generation for Open-Ended Document-Grounded QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19685)
*Saransh Sharma,Pritika Ramu,Aparna Garimella,Koyel Mukherjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"文档相关的洞察生成"新任务，旨在通过从文档集合中生成额外洞察来帮助改进、扩展或重新思考开放性问题的初始答案。同时发布了包含3000个问题和20个研究集合的数据集SCOpE-QA，并提出两阶段方法InsightGen，通过聚类构建主题表示，再基于主题图选择相关上下文生成洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI系统回答开放性问题的挑战：(1) 需要超越事实检索的合成、判断和探索能力；(2) 用户通常通过多次迭代来完善答案；(3) 现有QA基准测试没有明确支持这种改进过程。因此需要新的任务和评估范式来支持更丰富的用户交互和更好的问答体验。

Method: InsightGen两阶段方法：阶段一使用聚类技术构建文档集合的主题表示；阶段二基于主题图的邻域选择机制选取相关上下文，然后利用大语言模型(LLM)生成多样且相关的洞察。

Result: 在3000个问题上使用两个生成模型和两种评估设置进行广泛评估，结果表明InsightGen持续产生有用、相关且可操作的洞察，成功为该新任务建立了强有力的基线。

Conclusion: 本文成功定义了文档相关的洞察生成任务，发布了大规模数据集SCOpE-QA，并验证了InsightGen方法的有效性。该工作为支持用户在开放性问答中的迭代改进提供了新的研究方向和评估基准。

Abstract: Answering open-ended questions remains challenging for AI systems because it requires synthesis, judgment, and exploration beyond factual retrieval, and users often refine answers through multiple iterations rather than accepting a single response. Existing QA benchmarks do not explicitly support this refinement process. To address this gap, we introduce a new task, document-grounded related insight generation, where the goal is to generate additional insights from a document collection that help improve, extend, or rethink an initial answer to an open-ended question, ultimately supporting richer user interaction and a better overall question answering experience. We curate and release SCOpE-QA (Scientific Collections for Open-Ended QA), a dataset of 3,000 open-ended questions across 20 research collections. We present InsightGen, a two-stage approach that first constructs a thematic representation of the document collection using clustering, and then selects related context based on neighborhood selection from the thematic graph to generate diverse and relevant insights using LLMs. Extensive evaluation on 3,000 questions using two generation models and two evaluation settings shows that InsightGen consistently produces useful, relevant, and actionable insights, establishing a strong baseline for this new task.

</details>


### [72] [Discovering a Shared Logical Subspace: Steering LLM Logical Reasoning via Alignment of Natural-Language and Symbolic Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19716)
*Feihao Fang,My T. Thai,Yuanyuan Lei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出假设：LLM中存在一个共享的内部逻辑子空间，可以同时对齐自然语言和符号语言的推理视图。通过CCA分析配对的残差激活来学习低维子空间，并设计无训练方法来引导推理链。实验在4个逻辑推理基准上验证，准确率提升达11个百分点，且具有良好的跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多步逻辑推理任务中仍表现不佳。现有方法要么仅在自然语言形式上优化推理链，要么外挂符号求解器作为外部模块，缺乏对LLM内部逻辑表征的深入探索。

Method: 1) 假设LLM中存在逻辑子空间，能同时捕捉自然语言和符号语言推理的共享逻辑能力，独立于表面形式；2) 使用Canonical Correlation Analysis对配对的残差激活进行分析，学习最大跨视图相关性的低维子空间；3) 设计无训练方法，沿此逻辑子空间引导LLM推理链。

Result: 在4个逻辑推理基准上的实验表明：准确率提升最高达11个百分点，且在领域外问题上具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过揭示和利用LLM内部逻辑子空间，可以有效整合自然语言与符号语言的互补推理信号，为提升LLM逻辑推理能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) still struggle with multi-step logical reasoning. Existing approaches either purely refine the reasoning chain in natural language form or attach a symbolic solver as an external module. In this work, we instead ask whether LLMs contain a shared internal logical subspace that simultaneously aligns natural-language and symbolic-language views of the reasoning process. Our hypothesis is that this logical subspace captures logical reasoning capabilities in LLMs that are shared across views while remaining independent of surface forms. To verify this, we employ Canonical Correlation Analysis on the paired residual activations from natural-language and symbolic-language reasoning chains, learning a low-dimensional subspace with maximum cross-view correlation. Furthermore, we design a training-free approach that steers LLMs reasoning chain along this logical subspace, thereby leveraging the complementary reasoning signals from both views. Experiments on four logical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, improving accuracy by up to 11 percentage points and generalizing well on out-of-domain problems.

</details>

### [73] [Coordinatewise Balanced Covering for Linear Gain Graphs, with an Application to Coset-List Min-2-Lin over Powers of Two](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18661)
*Faruk Alpay,Levent Sarioglu*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在2的幂次模数域上变量受限的约束满足问题的最小删除求解，通过将单坐标平衡覆盖定理张量化扩展到多坐标情形，并利用秩压缩技术降低问题复杂度，最终给出了一种随机化单边错误算法，时间复杂度为 2^(O(k^2ρ+k log(kρ+2)))·n^(O(1)) + O~(md+ρ^ω)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索列表约束模方程删除问题的一个中间地带——介于无列表情形和完全保守列表情形之间。具体研究对象是限制变量取值于 dyadic 陪集、二元约束为等式、负等式或倍增关系的 Coset-List Min-2-Lin± 问题，该问题在参数化复杂性理论中具有重要地位。

Method: 证明方法的核心是将Dabrowski等人关于单个坐标的平衡覆盖定理通过张量积操作扩展到多个坐标，并结合秩压缩定理用固有循环标签秩ρ取代环境提升维度。此外还发展了循环空间形式化、割空间/势函数平衡性刻画、等价标签的最小维度陈述以及 dyadic coset 系统的显式位提升分析。

Result: 主要技术成果是线性增益图的坐标方向平衡覆盖定理：对于任意成本至多k的平衡子图，随机化算法以概率 2^(-O(k^2 r)) 输出顶点集S和边集F，使得(G-F)[S]保持平衡，且每个成本至多k的隐藏平衡子图被包含在S中，所有相关删除被F捕获。算法时间复杂度为 2^(O(k^2ρ + k log(kρ+2)))·n^(O(1)) + O~(md + ρ^ω)，可找到所有规模至多k的解中的最小权重可行删除集。

Conclusion: 该研究通过坐标方向的平衡覆盖定理和秩压缩技术，为列表约束的模方程删除问题提供了有效的算法框架，将问题复杂度从环境提升维度降低到固有循环标签秩ρ，实现了从无列表情形向完全保守列表情形的中间地带的突破。

Abstract: We study a list-constrained extension of modular equation deletion over powers of two, called Coset-List Min-2-Lin$^{\pm}$ over $\mathbb{Z}/2^d\mathbb{Z}$. Each variable is restricted to a dyadic coset $a+2^{\ell}(\mathbb{Z}/2^d\mathbb{Z})$, each binary constraint is of the form $x_u=x_v$, $x_u=-x_v$, or $x_u=2x_v$, and the goal is to delete a minimum number of constraints so that the remaining system is satisfiable. This problem lies between the no-list case and the poorly understood fully conservative list setting.
  Our main technical result is a coordinatewise balanced covering theorem for linear gain graphs labeled by vectors in $\mathbb{F}_2^r$. Given any balanced subgraph of cost at most $k$, a randomized procedure outputs a vertex set $S$ and an edge set $F$ such that $(G-F)[S]$ is balanced and, with probability $2^{-O(k^2r)}$, every hidden balanced subgraph of cost at most $k$ is contained in $S$ while all incident deletions are captured by $F$. The proof tensors the one-coordinate balanced-covering theorem of Dabrowski, Jonsson, Ordyniak, Osipov, and Wahlström across coordinates, and is combined with a rank-compression theorem replacing the ambient lifted dimension by the intrinsic cycle-label rank $ρ$.
  We also develop a cycle-space formulation, a cut-space/potential characterization of balancedness, a minimal-dimension statement for equivalent labelings, and an explicit bit-lifting analysis for dyadic coset systems. These yield a randomized one-sided-error algorithm running in \[ 2^{O(k^2ρ+k\log(kρ+2))}\cdot n^{O(1)}+\widetilde{O}(md+ρ^ω), \] and the same framework returns a minimum-weight feasible deletion set among all solutions of size at most $k$.

</details>


### [74] [Faster Linear-Space Data Structures for Path Frequency Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18667)
*Ovidiu Rata*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出线性空间数据结构，用于树上路径频率查询（路径众数、路径最不频繁元素、路径α-少数元素及路径最大g值颜色查询）。在词-RAM模型下，预处理时间O(n√(nw))，路径众数和路径最不频繁元素查询时间改进至O(√(n/w))，路径α-少数元素查询时间改进至O(α⁻¹)，并首次提出路径最大g值颜色查询的线性空间数据结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性空间数据结构在路径频率查询上存在查询时间瓶颈。Durocher等人(2016)的结构虽采用线性空间，但查询时间为O(log log n √(n/w))，仍有优化空间。本文旨在构建更高效的线性空间数据结构以提升查询性能。

Method: 采用随机化算法结合分解技术。对于路径众数和路径最不频繁元素查询，通过改进算法复杂度实现查询时间从O(log log n √(n/w))降至O(√(n/w))；对于路径α-少数元素查询，采用简单随机算法使查询时间从O(α⁻¹ log log n)降至O(α⁻¹)，成功概率≥1/2。

Result: 首次实现路径众数和路径最不频繁元素的线性空间数据结构，查询时间O(√(n/w))；将路径α-少数元素查询时间从O(α⁻¹ log log n)降至O(α⁻¹)；提出首个支持路径最大g值颜色查询的线性空间数据结构，预处理O(n√(nw))，查询O(√(n/w))。

Conclusion: 本文在词-RAM模型(w∈Ω(log n))下，通过随机化算法显著提升了树上路径频率查询的效率，所有数据结构均保持线性空间，为该领域提供了更优的理论性能界限。

Abstract: We present linear-space data structures for several frequency queries on trees, namely: path mode, path least frequent element, and path $α$-minority queries. We present the first linear-space data structures, requiring $O(n \sqrt{nw})$ preprocessing time, that can answer path mode and path least frequent element queries in $O(\sqrt{n/w})$ time. This improves upon the best previously known bound of $O(\log\log n \sqrt{n/w})$ achieved by Durocher et al. in 2016.
  For the path $α$-minority problem, where $α$ is specified at query time, we reduce the query time of the linear-space data structure of Durocher et al. from $O(α^{-1}\log\log n)$ down to $O(α^{-1})$ by employing a simple randomized algorithm with a success probability $\geq 1/2$.
  We also present the first linear-space data structure supporting "Path Maximum $g$-value Color" queries in $O(\sqrt{n/w})$ time, requiring $O(n \sqrt{nw})$ preprocessing time. This general framework encapsulates both path mode and path least frequent element queries. For our data structures, we consider the word-RAM model with $w\in Ω(\log n)$, where $w$ is the word size in bits.

</details>


### [75] [Parameterized Capacitated Vertex Cover Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18746)
*Michael Lampis,Manolis Vasilakis*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文对硬容量顶点覆盖问题进行了精细的参数化复杂度分析，在多个标准参数（自然参数k、顶点覆盖数vc、树宽tw、团宽）下给出了几乎最优的算法下界和上界，揭示了已知算法在这些参数下是本质最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬容量顶点覆盖是参数化复杂度领域的经典基准问题，此前已被证明是树宽参数化下W[1]难的。该文旨在重新审视其精确复杂度，获得更精细的参数化下界。

Method: 通过下界约化（ETH下界、最优性归约）和N-折叠整数规划算法设计，给出问题在不同参数下的精确复杂度刻画。

Result: 对于自然参数k，证明了不存在$k^{o(k)}n^{O(1)}$算法；顶点覆盖数参数下给出$2^{O(vc^{2-ε})}$下界障碍；树宽参数下证明$n^{O(tw)}$下界即使对树深度参数化也成立；并证明CVC在团宽6的图上已是NP难的。

Conclusion: 证明了硬容量顶点覆盖问题的精确复杂度下界，为结构参数化复杂性的细粒度分析提供了更清晰的图景。

Abstract: Capacitated Vertex Cover is the hard-capacitated variant of Vertex Cover: given a graph, a capacity for every vertex, and an integer $k$, the task is to select at most $k$ vertices that cover all edges and assign each edge to one of its chosen endpoints so that no chosen vertex receives more incident edges than its capacity. This problem is a classical benchmark in parameterized complexity, as it was among the first natural problems shown to be W[1]-hard when parameterized by treewidth. We revisit its exact complexity from a fine-grained parameterized perspective and obtain a much sharper picture for several standard parameters. For the natural parameter $k$, we prove under the Exponential Time Hypothesis (ETH) that no algorithm with running time $k^{o(k)} n^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$ exists. In particular, this shows that the known algorithms with running time $k^{\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{tw})} n^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$ are essentially optimal. We then turn to more general structural parameters. For vertex cover number $\mathrm{vc}$, we give evidence against a $2^{\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{vc}^{2-\varepsilon})} n^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$ algorithm, as such an improvement would imply corresponding progress for a broader class of integer-programming-type problems. We complement this barrier with a nearly matching upper bound for vertex integrity $\mathrm{vi}$, improving the previously known double-exponential dependence to an algorithm with running time $\mathrm{vi}^{\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{vi}^{2})} n^{\mathcal{O}(1)}$ using $N$-fold integer programming. For treewidth, we show that the standard dynamic programming algorithm with running time $n^{\mathcal{O}(\mathrm{tw})}$ is essentially optimal under the ETH, even if one parameterizes by tree-depth. Turning to clique-width, we prove that Capacitated Vertex Cover remains NP-hard already on graphs of linear clique-width $6$...

</details>


### [76] [Moderately beyond clique-width: reduced component max-leaf and related parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19138)
*Édouard Bonnet,Yeonsu Chang,Julien Duron,Colin Geniet,O-joung Kwon*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文在[BKW, JCTB '26; BKRT, SODA '22]的约化参数框架下，引入了新的图参数——约化组件最大叶子数(cml^↓)，证明其在单位区间图中是有界的，在平面图中是无界的。基于此设计了多项式时间算法，并建立了若干稀疏类的结构性质崩溃定理（如有界最大度蕴含有界树宽），同时利用约化参数证明了有界p^↓的图类族在一次逻辑转导下封闭，并重新证明了平面图不能一次阶逻辑转导三维网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要统一的参数框架来理解图类上问题的难解性边界。现有参数如团宽、带宽等各有局限，需在约化参数框架下引入新参数以更好地刻画平面图、稀疏图等重要图类的结构性质，并回答[BKW '26]中关于三维网格的约化带宽的开放问题。

Method: 基于收缩序列(contraction sequences)定义约化组件最大叶子数cml^↓；利用平衡分割定理证明有界cml^↓蕴含有界树宽；通过逻辑转导(argument from closure properties)建立不可转导性结果。

Result: (1) cml^↓严格介于团宽与约化带宽之间；(2) 在单位区间图中有界，平面图中无界；(3) 给出Max Induced d-Regular Subgraph和Induced Disjoint Paths在低cml^↓下的多项式算法；(4) 有界cml^↓的稀疏类中，有界最大度→有界树宽，K_{t,t}-无子图→强次线性树宽（更一般地对有界约化割宽成立）；(5) 大多数约化参数p^↓的类在一次阶逻辑转导下封闭；(6) 三维网格的约化带宽是无界的。

Conclusion: 约化组件最大叶子数cml^↓统一并推广了有界团宽和单位区间图类上的可解性结果；建立了有界约化参数类的转导封闭性理论框架；回答了[BKW '26]的开放问题，并重新证明了平面图不能一次阶逻辑转导三维网格这一近期结果[GPP, LICS '25]。

Abstract: Reduced parameters [BKW, JCTB '26; BKRT, SODA '22] are defined via contraction sequences. Based on this framework, we introduce the reduced component max-leaf, denoted by $\operatorname{cml}^\downarrow$, where component max-leaf is the maximum number of leaves in any spanning tree of any connected component. Reduced component max-leaf is strictly sandwiched between clique-width and reduced bandwidth, it is bounded in unit interval graphs, and unbounded in planar graphs. We design polynomial-time algorithms for problems such as \textsc{Maximum Induced $d$-Regular Subgraph} and \textsc{Induced Disjoint Paths} in graphs given with a contraction sequence witnessing low $\operatorname{cml}^\downarrow$, unifying and extending tractability results for classes of bounded clique-width and unit interval graphs.
  We get the following collapses in sparse classes of bounded $\operatorname{cml}^\downarrow$: bounded maximum degree implies bounded treewidth, whereas $K_{t,t}$-subgraph-freeness implies strongly sublinear treewidth; we show the latter, more generally, for classes of bounded reduced cutwidth. We establish the former result by showing that graphs with bounded $\operatorname{cml}^\downarrow$ admit balanced separators dominated by a bounded number of vertices. We then showcase an application of the reduced parameters to establishing non-transducibility results. We prove that for most reduced parameters $p^\downarrow$ (including reduced bandwidth), the family of classes of bounded $p^\downarrow$ is closed under first-order transductions. We then answer a question of [BKW '26] by showing that the 3-dimensional grids have unbounded reduced bandwidth. As the class of planar graphs (or any class of bounded genus) has bounded reduced bandwidth [BKW '26], this reproves a recent result [GPP, LICS '25] that planar graphs do not first-order transduce the 3-dimensional grids.

</details>


### [77] [Effective Traveling for Metric Instances of the Traveling Thief Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19271)
*Jan Eube,Kelin Luo,Aneta Neumann,Frank Neumann,Heiko Röglin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究旅行窃贼问题（TTP）的旅游优化分量，在固定装箱方案下将其表述为加权旅行商问题（TSP），其中旅行成本依赖于已收集物品的累计重量。研究提出了路径度量下的一般代价函数O(n²)动态规划算法，证明了星度量下的NP难解性，并分别为星度量提供常数因子近似算法和一般度量下线性代价函数提供近似算法。实验结果表明方法在标准TTP实例上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行窃贼问题（TTP）结合了旅行商问题（TSP）和背包问题（KP），是复杂的多组件优化问题。由于高度复杂的旅行分量导致有效的精确方法不可用，评判现有算法的性能——特别是与最优解的逼近程度——变得非常困难。

Method: 1. 将TTP的旅游优化分量表述为加权TSP变体，旅行成本取决于收集物品的累计重量；2. 对路径度量提出O(n²)时间复杂度的动态规划算法；3. 证明问题在星度量上为NP难；4. 为星度量开发常数因子近似算法；5. 为一般度量下线性代价函数开发近似算法；6. 在调整为路径度量的标准TTP实例上进行实验评估。

Result: 理论方面：提出了路径度量下的动态规划算法，证明了星度量上的NP难解性，并给出了星度量和一般度量下的近似算法。实验方面：方法在某些基准实例上显著提升了解的质量，同时指出某些迭代搜索方法所得解的旅游分量已是最优的。

Conclusion: 研究通过理论分析和实验验证，确立了加权TSP在旅游优化中的计算复杂性框架，为TTP的组件分解研究提供了重要见解。提出的动态规划和近似算法在实际TTP实例上展现有效性，为评估和优化TTP算法提供了新的工具。

Abstract: The Traveling Thief Problem (TTP) is a multi-component optimization problem that captures the interplay between routing and packing decisions by combining the classical Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP) and the Knapsack Problem (KP). The TTP has gained significant attention in the evolutionary computation literature and a wide range of approaches have been developed over the last 10 years. Judging the performance of these algorithms in particular in terms of how close the get to optimal solutions is a very challenging task as effective exact methods are not available due to the highly challenging traveling component. In this paper, we study the tour-optimization component of TTP under a fixed packing plan. We formulate this task as a weighted variant of the TSP, where travel costs depend on the cumulative weight of collected items, and investigate how different distance metrics and cost functions affect computational complexity. We present an $(O(n^2))$-time dynamic programming algorithm for the path metric with general cost functions, prove that the problem is NP-hard even on a star metric, and develop constant-factor approximation algorithms for star metrics. Finally, we also develop an approximation algorithm for the problem under a general metric with a linear cost function.
  We complement our theoretical results with experimental evaluations on standard TTP instances adjusted to a path metric. Our experimental results demonstrate the practical effectiveness of our approaches by comparing it to solutions produced by popular iterative search algorithms. The results show that our methods are able to significantly improve the quality of solutions for some benchmark instances by optimizing the traveling part while pointing out the optimality of the travel component for other solutions obtained by iterative search methods.

</details>


### [78] [Suffix Random Access via Function Inversion: A Key for Asymmetric Streaming String Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19371)
*Panagiotis Charalampopoulos,Taha El Ghazi,Jonas Ellert,Paweł Gawrychowski,Tatiana Starikovskaya*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文在不对称流模型下提出了将字符串处理问题规约到只读在线模型的一般性技术框架，核心工具是后缀随机访问数据结构（与密码学中的函数逆问题双向等价），并设计了局部稀疏的字符串同步集。应用包括精确/近似模式匹配和Lempel-Ziv压缩的高效流式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bridge the gap between classical string algorithms and streaming algorithms. While classical problems often have efficient solutions, streaming setting with sublinear workspace frequently lacks efficient solutions. The asymmetric streaming model (reference + streaming text) serves as an intermediate model that should inherit properties from both settings.

Method: 1) Propose suffix random access data structure maintaining constant-time access to longest suffix in reference; 2) Establish bidirectional reduction between suffix random access and cryptographic function inversion; 3) Develop locally sparse string synchronizing sets with efficient streaming construction; 4) Use these tools as building blocks for generic reduction framework.

Result: Achieved asymmetric streaming algorithms for: exact pattern matching, approximate pattern matching (Hamming distance), approximate pattern matching (edit distance), and relative Lempel-Ziv compression. All improvements generalize or lift existing algorithms.

Conclusion: The paper provides a generic reduction framework from asymmetric streaming model to online read-only model, with efficient implementations for pattern matching and compression. Key innovation is the suffix random access data structure, with interesting cryptographic connections via function inversion reduction.

Abstract: Many string processing problems can be phrased in the streaming setting, where the input arrives symbol by symbol and we have sublinear working space. The area of streaming algorithms for string processing has flourished since the seminal work of Porat and Porat [FOCS 2009].
  Unfortunately, problems with efficient solutions in the classical setting often do not admit efficient solutions in the streaming setting. As a bridge between these two settings, Saks and Seshadhri [SODA 2013] introduced the asymmetric streaming model. Here, one is given read-only access to a (typically short) reference string $R$ of length $m$, while a text $T$ arrives as a stream.
  We provide a generic technique to reduce fundamental string problems in the asymmetric streaming model to the online read-only model, lifting several existing algorithms and generally improving upon the state of the art. Most notably, we obtain asymmetric streaming algorithms for exact and approximate pattern matching (under both the Hamming and edit distances), and for relative Lempel-Ziv compression.
  At the heart of our approach lies a novel tool that facilitates efficient computation in the asymmetric streaming model: the suffix random access data structure. In its simplest variant, it maintains constant-time random access to the longest suffix of (the seen prefix of) $T$ that occurs in $R$. We show a bidirectional reduction between suffix random access and function inversion, a central problem in cryptography.
  On the way to our upper bound, we propose a variant of the string synchronizing sets ([Kempa and Kociumaka; STOC 2019]) with a local sparsity condition that, as we show, admits an efficient streaming construction algorithm. We believe that our framework and techniques will find broad applications in the development of small-space string algorithms.

</details>
