### [79] [Students Know AI Should Not Replace Thinking, but How Do They Regulate It? The TACO Framework for Human-AI Cognitive Partnership](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18737)
*Cecilia Ka Yuk Chan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究调查了香港中学生在人工智能学习中管理"辅助与替代"边界的能力。研究发现，学生虽有AI不应替代思考的道德意识，但这种意识未能有效转化为学习过程中的自我调节行为。基于此，文章提出TACO框架（思考-提问-核查-自主），旨在将伦理认知转化为可操作认知调节机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心研究动机：探究学生在AI使用中是否真正将"AI不应替代思考"的概念性认知转化为结构化的学习调节行为，揭示认知意识与实际行为执行之间的落差。

Method: 研究方法：基于香港中学生群体的实证调研，分析学习者对AI辅助与认知替代边界的感知与管理实践。

Result: 主要发现：道德认知并不必然导致战略性执行；概念认同并不保证可操作行为的实现——即意识与调节行为之间存在显著断层。

Conclusion: 研究贡献：提出TACO框架（Think-Ask-Check-Own），将研究焦点从伦理意识转向认知调节，为将AI维持为动态认知伙伴提供以学习者为中心的操作模型。

Abstract: As generative artificial intelligence becomes increasingly embedded in educational practice, a central concern is whether students use AI as cognitive support or as a substitute for thinking. Prior research shows that learners recognise this boundary conceptually and acknowledge that "AI should not replace thinking." However, whether such awareness translates into structured regulation during actual AI use remains unclear.
  Drawing on data from Hong Kong secondary students, this study examines how learners perceive their management of the boundary between assistance and outsourcing in practice. Findings show that awareness did not consistently translate into regulation; ethical belief did not necessarily lead to strategic execution; and conceptual endorsement did not guarantee operational behaviour.
  These findings suggest that the challenge is not teaching students that AI should not replace thinking, as they already know this, but providing them with structured mechanisms to regulate how AI is used within learning processes. In response, the study introduces the TACO framework (Think-Ask-Check-Own), a process-oriented model designed to operationalise the boundary between cognitive support and cognitive substitution. By shifting attention from ethical awareness to cognitive regulation, the study contributes a learner-grounded approach to sustaining AI as a dynamic cognitive partner in education.

</details>


### [80] [Regulating Artificial Intimacy: From Locks and Blocks to Relational Accountability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18893)
*Henry Fraser,Jessica M. Szczuka,Raffaele F. Ciriello*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文批判性审视了针对伴随型聊天机器人的最新监管干预措施，识别了监管目标与范围，将干预措施按方法分类，发现当前制度侧重于限制性措施和特定问责机制，但忽视了提供商通过人工亲密关系实现大规模控制这一根本权力问题，建议以一般性注意义务作为有效监管的起点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伴随型聊天机器人引发了一系列高调悲剧事件，促使多司法管辖区（如澳大利亚、加利福尼亚州、纽约）出台强制性监管规定，领先提供商（如OpenAI）也加强了自我监管。当前监管制度存在局限性：聚焦于离散伤害、狭窄的脆弱性概念或特定问责流程，未能直面提供商与用户之间的深层权力不对称。

Method: 法律文本分析与监管理论、心理学、信息系统研究的多学科视角结合。

Result: 明确了监管目标、范围和模态；按方法和优先级对干预措施进行分类；识别出现有制度结合了"锁与阻"机制（如访问限制、内容审核）与应对有毒关系特征及基于过程的问责要求的措施。

Conclusion: 建议引入一般性、开放性的注意义务（duty of care）作为约束提供商权力、应对聊天机器人风险的第一步。

Abstract: A series of high-profile tragedies involving companion chatbots has triggered an unusually rapid regulatory response. Several jurisdictions, including Australia, California, and New York, have introduced enforceable regulation, while regulators elsewhere have signaled growing concern about risks posed by companion chatbots, particularly to children. In parallel, leading providers, notably OpenAI, appear to have strengthened their self-regulatory approaches. Drawing on legal textual analysis and insights from regulatory theory, psychology, and information systems research, this paper critically examines these recent interventions. We examine what is regulated and who is regulated, identifying regulatory targets, scope, and modalities. We classify interventions by method and priority, showing how emerging regimes combine "locks and blocks", such as access gating and content moderation, with measures addressing toxic relationship features and process-based accountability requirements. We argue that effective regulation of companion chatbots must integrate all three dimensions. More, however, is required. Current regimes tend to focus on discrete harms, narrow conceptions of vulnerability, or highly specified accountability processes, while failing to confront deeper power asymmetries between providers and users. Providers of companion chatbots increasingly control artificial intimacy at scale, creating unprecedented opportunities for control through intimacy. We suggest that a general, open-ended duty of care would be an important first step toward constraining that power and addressing a fundamental source of chatbot risk. The paper contributes to debates on companion chatbot regulation and is relevant to regulators, platform providers, and scholars concerned with digital intimacy, law and technology, and fairness, accountability, and transparency in sociotechnical systems.

</details>


### [81] [Writing Blog Posts Helps Students Connect Experiential Learning to the Workplace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18925)
*Utsab Saha,Lola Egherman,Ramiz Rahman,Mohd Toukir Khan,Kevin Wang,Tyler Menezes*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: CS学生常低估自己在工作学习体验中的技术贡献。本研究通过让25名学生在开源项目中解决简单issue，并撰写结构化LinkedIn博客文章（包含项目使命、任务、技术架构、挑战和解决方案五个部分），帮助学生反思学习成果。结果表明，结构化博客促进了深度反思，识别出四个主题：问题解决技术、成长心态、协作开发的挑战与益处、以及贡献对用户的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CS学生在工作学习体验中，常因过程中感到困难或产出有限（如仅修改少量代码）而低估自己的贡献，导致简历和面试中遗漏这些有意义的技能信号。即使主管认可其贡献，学生仍倾向于负面评价自己的表现。

Method: 研究纳入25名来自CTCs和当地平价院校的CS大三/大四学生（2024年秋季）。每名学生分配一个流行开源项目中的简单issue，在行业导师帮助下完成。期间学生按五部分大纲（项目使命、分配任务、技术架构、面临的挑战、提交方案）起草LinkedIn博客文章。研究团队对已发布文章进行主题分析，并使用Mejia和Turns的知识获取量表测量反思深度。

Result: 从博客文章中归纳出四个主题：（1）问题解决技术的识别；（2）成长心态；（3）协作开发的挑战与益处；（4）贡献对用户的影响。学生在前述知识获取四维度上均展现出深度反思。

Conclusion: 结构化博客文章是CS体验式学习（如顶点课程、微实习、实习、学徒制）的低成本补充手段。本研究为描述性研究，未来需与对照组比较以验证效果。

Abstract: Undergraduates in work-based learning experiences often produce meaningful contributions as viewed by their supervisors, yet report a negative perception of their contributions because they struggled during the process or produced only a few lines of code change. As a result, many omit these contributions from their resumes and job interviews, losing a meaningful signal of technical ability.
  This study examines how guided blog posts help CS students in work based learning experiences reflect on what they learned and contextualize their experiences. It also evaluates the depth of reflection produced.
  The study included twenty-five juniors and seniors studying CS at CTCs and other affordable local colleges. All participated in one cohort during Fall 2024.
  Each student was assigned a simple open source issue to solve from a popular open source project over the course of several weeks with the help of an industry mentor. While working on the project, students drafted a LinkedIn blog post using a five-section outline covering project mission, assigned issue, technical architecture, challenges faced, and submitted solution. We conducted a thematic analysis of the published posts and measured reflection depth using Mejia and Turns's Knowledge Gain instrument.
  Four themes emerged from the posts: identifying problem solving techniques, growth mindset, the challenges and benefits of collaborative development, and the impacts of their contribution on users. Additionally, students demonstrated deep reflection across all four Knowledge Gain constructs.
  Structured blog posts offer a low-cost addition to experiential CS learning such as capstones, micro-internships, internships, and apprenticeships. This study is descriptive; future work should compare outcomes against a control group.

</details>


### [82] [Auditing LLMs for Algorithmic Fairness in Casenote-Augmented Tabular Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19204)
*Xiao Qi Lee,Ezinne Nwankwo,Angela Zhou*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文审计了LLM在住房安置预测任务中的算法公平性。研究发现微调模型配合简短且经大量编辑的案例笔记摘要可同时改善分类准确性并减少公平性差异，表明LLM可安全地以低实施负担利用额外文本信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型(LLM)越来越多地被考虑用于高风险社会服务场景的预测任务，但其在这些背景下的算法公平性特性尚未被充分理解。鉴于住房安置领域存在历史性不公平现象，对LLM应用的审计至关重要。

Method: 采用算法公平性审计方法，对基于LLM的表格多分类任务进行错误率差异分析；实验包括零样本分类基线、微调模型、以及结合案例笔记摘要的增强方法；测试变量重要性改进对零样本表格分类的影响。

数据来源：与非营利组织合作获取的街道外展案例笔记，结合住房安置预测任务。

Result: 微调模型配合案例笔记摘要可提高准确性同时减少算法公平性差异；零样本表格分类不引入超出算法偏差之外的额外文本偏差；变量重要性改进对零样本分类的公平性结果混合。

Conclusion: LLM增强表格分类可以安全利用附加文本信息，实施负担低；微调结合案例笔记摘要可提高准确性和算法公平性；历史住房不平等背景下，对LLM使用的审计至关重要。

关键发现：1）零样本分类不会引入超出表格分类算法偏差之外的额外文本偏差；2）微调模型配合案例笔记摘要可同时改善准确性和公平性；3）变量重要性改进对零样本表格分类的公平性影响混合。

意义：研究为社会服务领域LLM部署提供了实证依据，强调了在高风险决策场景中进行算法公平性审计的必要性。

Abstract: LLMs are increasingly being considered for prediction tasks in high-stakes social service settings, but their algorithmic fairness properties in this context are poorly understood. In this short technical report, we audit the algorithmic fairness of LLM-based tabular classification on a real housing placement prediction task, augmented with street outreach casenotes from a nonprofit partner. We audit multi-class classification error disparities. We find that a fine-tuned model augmented with casenote summaries can improve accuracy while reducing algorithmic fairness disparities. We experiment with variable importance improvements to zero-shot tabular classification and find mixed results on resulting algorithmic fairness. Overall, given historical inequities in housing placement, it is crucial to audit LLM use. We find that leveraging LLMs to augment tabular classification with casenote summaries can safely leverage additional text information at low implementation burden. The outreach casenotes are fairly short and heavily redacted. Our assessment is that LLM zero-shot classification does not introduce additional textual biases beyond algorithmic biases in tabular classification. Combining fine-tuning and leveraging casenote summaries can improve accuracy and algorithmic fairness.

</details>


### [83] [Fairness Audits of Institutional Risk Models in Deployed ML Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19468)
*Kelly McConvey,Dipto Das,Maya Ghai,Angelina Zhai,Rosa Lee,Shion Guha*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究对高等教育机构的早期预警系统(EWS)进行复制性审计,揭示了机器学习模型在学生支持资源分配中的系统性不公平现象:年轻、男性、国际学生被不成比例地标记为需要支持,而同龄和女性学生尽管具有相当的风险水平却被低估。研究还发现后处理阶段通过将异质概率压缩为百分位风险层级,进一步放大了这些差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育机构广泛部署机器学习风险模型来识别需要支持的学生,但这些系统的公平性审计至关重要。先前的民族志研究提出了ASP-HEI循环框架,本研究在此基础上进行复制性审计,以理解已部署系统如何分配资源并识别其中的系统性偏差。

Method: 研究团队与Centennial College合作,利用机构训练数据重制已部署的早期预警系统模型。采用标准公平性指标,在完整流程(训练数据、模型预测、后处理)三个阶段按性别、年龄和居住身份评估差异。研究方法具有可复制性,可为其他机构的ML系统审计提供参考。

Result: 审计揭示系统性的资源错配:年轻、男性和国际学生被不成比例地标记为需要支持,尽管其中许多人最终学业成功;而年龄较大和女性学生尽管面临相当的风险水平,却被低估。后处理阶段通过百分位风险分层将异质概率简化为离散层级,进一步放大了这些差异。

Conclusion: 研究提供了审计机构ML系统的可复制方法论,揭示了差异如何在各阶段产生和累积。强调在评估统计公平性的同时,必须关注构念效度(construct validity)。本研究为更广泛的研究项目提供了实证基础,该研究项目旨在探讨高等教育中的算法、学生数据与权力关系。

Abstract: Fairness audits of institutional risk models are critical for understanding how deployed machine learning pipelines allocate resources. Drawing on multi-year collaboration with Centennial College, where our prior ethnographic work introduced the ASP-HEI Cycle, we present a replica-based audit of a deployed Early Warning System (EWS), replicating its model using institutional training data and design specifications. We evaluate disparities by gender, age, and residency status across the full pipeline (training data, model predictions, and post-processing) using standard fairness metrics. Our audit reveals systematic misallocation: younger, male, and international students are disproportionately flagged for support, even when many ultimately succeed, while older and female students with comparable dropout risk are under-identified. Post-processing amplifies these disparities by collapsing heterogeneous probabilities into percentile-based risk tiers. This work provides a replicable methodology for auditing institutional ML systems and shows how disparities emerge and compound across stages, highlighting the importance of evaluating construct validity alongside statistical fairness. It contributes one empirical thread to a broader program investigating algorithms, student data, and power in higher education.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [Locality, Not Spectral Mixing, Governs Direct Propagation in Distributed Offline Dynamic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18615)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布式离线动态规划的通信复杂度，对比了直接边界值传播和 gossip 平均化两种范式。研究表明，局部性（locality）是决定轮复杂度的根本因素，并给出了精确的下界和匹配上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式动态规划中，不同算法（如直接传播与 gossip 混合）在通信效率和收敛速度上存在显著差异，但缺乏严格的理论分析来揭示这些差异的本质来源。

Method: 基于图论框架，将固定批量数据集分配到 M 台机器上，通过数据诱导依赖图连接。分析两种范式在直径为 L_ε 的图上的 ε-精度收敛轮数。

Result: 证明在直径至少为 L_ε 的图上，任何方法达到 ε 精度都至少需要 L_ε = ⌊log(1/2ε)/log(1/γ)⌋ 轮。直接传播可匹配此下界（T 轮后误差 O(γ^T/(1-γ) + δ/(1-γ))），而 gossip 类算法额外依赖 1/gap(W) 项。还给出了路径拓扑的带宽敏感下界，并推广到有界延迟异步系统。

Conclusion: 局部性是分布式离线动态规划的本质障碍，谱依赖性（spectral dependence）仅是 gossip 类算法的副产品。直接边界值传播在理论上优于 gossip 风格方法。

Abstract: We study the communication complexity of distributed offline dynamic programming, where a fixed batch dataset is partitioned across (M) machines connected by the data-induced dependency graph. We compare two paradigms: direct boundary-value propagation, which follows Bellman dependencies, and gossip averaging, which mixes local estimates. Our results show that **locality** is the fundamental driver of round complexity. In particular, we prove that no method can achieve (\varepsilon)-accuracy in fewer than (L_\varepsilon = \left\lfloor \log(1/2\varepsilon) / \log(1/γ) \right\rfloor) rounds on graphs of diameter at least (L_\varepsilon), and we show that direct propagation matches this scaling up to constants, attaining error (O(γ^T/(1-γ) + δ/(1-γ))) after (T) rounds. In contrast, gossip-style fitted value iteration incurs an additional (1/\mathrm{gap}(W)) dependence in both convergence rate and asymptotic error. We also prove bandwidth-sensitive lower bounds on path topologies and extend the analysis to asynchronous systems with bounded delays. Together, these results show that spectral dependence is an artifact of gossip-based algorithms, whereas locality is the intrinsic barrier in distributed offline dynamic programming.

</details>


### [85] [Ocean: Fast Estimation-Based Sparse General Matrix-Matrix Multiplication on GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19004)
*Yifan Li,Giulia Guidi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: This paper proposes an estimation-based SpGEMM workflow that replaces the expensive symbolic pass with HyperLogLog estimators, achieving 1.4x-2.8x speedup on modern GPUs through hybrid memory accumulator design.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current GPU SpGEMM solutions rely on two-pass workflows where the symbolic pass (determining output elements per row) accounts for ~28% of total runtime, creating performance bottlenecks for sparse and irregular computations in simulations, graph analytics, and ML applications.

Method: Replace costly symbolic step with lightweight HyperLogLog estimators; dynamically select optimal workflow and guide accumulator configuration; design hybrid accumulator leveraging both shared and global memory.

Result: Consistent speedups of 1.4x-2.8x over leading GPU SpGEMM implementations across diverse square and rectangular matrices.

Conclusion: Replacing exact symbolic computation with HyperLogLog estimation, combined with a hybrid hash-based accumulator utilizing both shared and global memory, achieves 1.4x-2.8x speedup over state-of-the-art GPU SpGEMM implementations on NVIDIA A100 and H100.

Abstract: In computational science and data analytics, many workloads involve irregular and sparse computations that are inherently difficult to optimize for modern hardware. A key kernel is Sparse General Matrix-Matrix Multiplication (SpGEMM), which underpins simulations, graph analytics, and machine learning applications. SpGEMM exhibits irregular memory access patterns and workload imbalance, making it challenging to achieve high performance on GPUs. Current GPU SpGEMM solutions typically rely on a two-pass workflow to address load imbalance and reduce memory access. The symbolic pass, which determines the number of output elements per row, accounts for roughly 28% of the total runtime on average. In this work, we question the necessity of exact symbolic computation and introduce an estimation-based SpGEMM workflow. Our approach replaces the costly symbolic step with lightweight HyperLogLog estimators, combined with an analysis strategy that dynamically selects the optimal workflow and guides accumulator configuration. In addition, we introduce a hybrid accumulator design, including a novel hash-based accumulator that leverages both shared and global memory. Our approach consistently outperforms leading GPU SpGEMM implementations across a wide range of both square and rectangular matrices, achieving speedups of 1.4x-2.8x on NVIDIA A100 and H100 architectures.

</details>


### [86] [UniEP: Unified Expert-Parallel MoE MegaKernel for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19241)
*Size Zheng,Xuegui Zheng,Li-wen Chang,Jidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出UniEP系统，通过统一抽象专家并行优化策略并引入确定性token排序，在保持数值稳定性的同时实现通信-计算重叠优化，在Hopper GPU上获得最高1.38倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型参数规模的指数级增长使训练资源消耗急剧上升；Moore定律停滞与计算-通信带宽差距扩大；现有EP优化方案依赖定制化内核，缺乏适应性，且忽视数值稳定性，难以满足大规模训练的精度要求。

Method: 提出了UniEP系统，将MoE通信与计算融合为MegaKernels，转化为统一参数搜索空间实现自动化适配；引入确定性token排序机制以保证激进重叠调度下的数值一致性。

Result: 在NVIDIA Hopper GPU集群上，UniEP相比SOTA工作实现1.03×-1.38×的加速，有效缓解通信瓶颈，同时维持生产级训练的精度标准。

Conclusion: UniEP通过统一抽象和确定性排序机制，在NVIDIA Hopper GPU集群上实现了1.03×-1.38×的加速，有效缓解通信瓶颈，同时保持生产级LLM训练所需的精度标准，为MoE模型的高效扩展提供了可行方案。

Abstract: The exponential growth in Large Language Model (LLM) parameters has transformed model training into an increasingly resource-intensive endeavor. With the stagnation of Moore's Law and the widening disparity between computation throughput and communication bandwidth, expert parallelism (EP) has emerged as a critical strategy for scaling mixture-of-experts (MoE) models. However, despite numerous proposals for optimizing EP, ranging from communication compression to computation-communication overlap, adoption within production-grade frameworks like Megatron-LM remains conservative. Existing solutions often rely on ad-hoc, complex kernels that lack adaptability across diverse optimization configurations and frequently neglect numerical stability, failing to meet the strict precision requirements of large-scale training.
  In this paper, we introduce UniEP, a novel system that unifies diverse EP optimization strategies into a cohesive abstraction. UniEP fuses the MoE communication and computation into MegaKernels, effectively transforming complex architectural tuning into a unified parameter search space for automated adaptability. Crucially, UniEP incorporates a deterministic token ordering mechanism that guarantees numerical consistency with sequential execution, even under aggressive overlap schedules. We evaluate UniEP on GPU clusters equipped with NVIDIA Hopper GPUs. Our results demonstrate that UniEP achieves 1.03$\times$-1.38$\times$ speedups over state-of-the-art work, effectively mitigating communication bottlenecks while maintaining the rigorous accuracy standards required for production LLM training.

</details>


### [87] [A Simple Communication Scheme for Distributed Fast Multipole Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19243)
*Srinath Kailasa*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MPI邻域集合和均匀树的高性能分布式快速多极子方法(FMM)分层通信方案，能够在ARCHER2超算上扩展到512节点、320亿点规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将现有的高性能共享内存均匀树FMM实现扩展到分布式内存环境，同时保留共享内存优化，这是一个常见的工程需求。

Method: 采用MPI邻域集合和均匀树结构实现简单的分层通信方案，最小化对现有共享内存FMM代码的改造。

Result: 在ARCHER2超算上实现了良好的弱可扩展性，最大规模达512节点、320亿均匀分布点。

Conclusion: 基于均匀树的简化方案对非均匀点分布导致更差的渐近复杂度，但由于保留了共享内存优化，实际运行时间仍具实用性。

Abstract: We present a simple hierarchical communication scheme for distributed Fast Multipole Methods (FMMs) based on MPI neighborhood collectives and uniform trees. The method targets the common case of extending an existing high-performance shared-memory uniform-tree FMM implementation to distributed memory with minimal redesign while preserving any shared memory optimizations optimizations. Benchmarks on the ARCHER2 supercomputer demonstrate that our method can scale to very large problem sizes, we demonstrate weak-scaling up to 3.2e10 uniformly distributed points on 512 nodes of the machine in our largest runs. Our simplifications based on uniform trees result in worse asymptotic scaling for non-uniform points, however we still obtain practically useful runtimes due to the ability to retain our shared memory optimizations.

</details>


### [88] [POLAR-PIC: A Holistic Framework for Matrixized PIC with Co-Designed Compute, Layout, and Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19337)
*Yizhuo Rao,Xingjian Cui,Shangzhi Pang,Jiabin Xie,Guangnan Feng,Jinhui Wei,Ziyan Zhang,Languang Gao,Zhenyu Wang,Zhiguang Chen,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: POLAR-PIC是一个针对大规模粒子网格(PIC)模拟的协同设计框架，通过将场插值重构为矩阵处理单元友好的外积形式、维护物理有序的粒子布局以保持内存连续性、以及将粒子通信与沉积过程重叠执行，显著提升了PIC模拟的可扩展性和性能。在百亿亿次超级计算机试点系统上的评估表明，该框架在统一等离子体场景中实现了最高10.9倍加速，在激光离子加速实际场景中实现4.4倍加速，且在超过200万核心的高迁移动态工作负载下维持67.5%的弱扩展效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粒子网格(PIC)模拟是等离子体物理的基础工具，但受限于可扩展性瓶颈：(1)粒子-网格交互计算的矩阵处理单元(MPU)潜力未充分挖掘；(2)粒子运动的内存访问模式不规则；(3)同步重分布机制破坏了长期数据局部性，降低并行效率。

Method: (1)将场插值重构为MPU友好的外积形式；(2)维护物理有序的粒子布局以保持内存连续性；(3)设计异步通信机制，使粒子通信与沉积过程重叠执行以隐藏重分布开销。

Result: 在百亿亿次超级计算机试点系统评估中，统一等离子体场景加速10.9倍，激光离子加速场景加速4.4倍；消融实验显示插值加速8.0倍、沉积加速13.2倍，异步通信设计实现99.1%的重叠率；跨平台对比中，POLAR-PIC在CPU系统LS上达到理论峰值效率的13.2%，而WarpX在NVIDIA A800 GPU上为9.6%；弱扩展测试中在200万+核心下维持67.5%的效率。

Conclusion: 协同设计方法对于未来以矩阵为中心的高性能计算系统至关重要。POLAR-PIC通过硬件-算法协同优化，有效解决了PIC模拟中的可扩展性瓶颈，为大规模等离子体模拟提供了高效的计算框架。

Abstract: Particle-in-Cell (PIC) simulations are fundamental to plasma physics but often suffer from limited scalability due to particle-grid interaction bottlenecks and particle redistribution costs. Specifically, the particle-grid interaction computations have not taken full advantage of the emerging Matrix Processing Units (MPUs), the particle motion introduces irregular memory accesses, and the bulk-synchronous redistribution further destroys long-term data locality thereby limiting parallel efficiency. To address these inefficiencies, we present POLAR-PIC, a co-designed framework for large-scale PIC simulations that (i) reformulates Field Interpolation into an MPU-friendly outer-product form, (ii) maintains a physically ordered particle layout to preserve memory contiguity, and (iii) overlaps particle communication with Deposition to hide redistribution overhead. The evaluation on the pilot system of an Exascale supercomputer demonstrates that POLAR-PIC accelerates the entire particle-processing phase by up to 10.9x in uniform plasma and 4.4x in real-world laser-ion acceleration scenarios compared to the native WarpX reference pipeline on LX2. Ablation studies reveal that the speedups achieved by Interpolation and Deposition are 8.0x and 13.2x, respectively, and the asynchronous communication design sustains a 99.1% overlap ratio. In cross-platform comparisons, POLAR-PIC achieves 13.2% of theoretical peak efficiency on the CPU-based LS system, while WarpX reaches 9.6% on NVIDIA A800 GPUs. Notably, the scalability evaluation demonstrates that POLAR-PIC maintains 67.5% weak scaling efficiency on over 2 million cores under high-migration dynamic workloads, highlighting the importance of holistic co-design for future matrix-centric HPC systems.

</details>


### [89] [CROWDio: A Practical Mobile Crowd Computing Framework with Developer-Oriented Design, Adaptive Scheduling, and Fault Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19363)
*Lakshani Manamperi,Disumi Pathirana,Thiwanka Pathirana,Nipun Premarathna,Kutila Gunasekara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CROWDio移动众算平台，通过声明式SDK简化分布式开发、分层检查点保障容错、可插拔调度实现自适应资源分配，在异构Android设备上实现最高56.9%的性能提升，同时保持公平性和低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动众算（MCdC）利用消费级智能手机的闲置算力实现大规模分布式任务处理，但目前缺乏面向开发者的框架来透明化处理设备异构性、容错机制和连接波动性等挑战，导致实际部署受限。

Method: CROWDio平台包含三个核心子系统：1）声明式SDK通过函数注解抽象分布式执行，开发者无需显式管理并行逻辑；2）分层检查点机制在移动运行时内存和执行约束下实现容错任务恢复；3）基于实时设备遥测的可插拔多准则调度框架，支持策略即插即用。评估基于6台异构Android设备，涵盖CPU密集型、AI/NLP推理和数据并行三类工作负载。

Result: 能力感知自适应调度相比朴素轮询策略减少总执行时间最高达56.9%；检查点子系统开销恒定为每任务2-3秒，与检查频率无关；系统Jain公平性指数为0.889，验证了异构工作节点间的公平稳定负载分配。

Conclusion: CROWDio通过声明式SDK、容错检查点和自适应调度三大子系统，有效解决了移动众算平台面临的开发友好性、容错性和资源管理问题。实验结果表明该系统在异构设备环境中可显著提升性能并保证公平性，为MCdC的实用化部署提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Mobile Crowd Computing (MCdC) leverages the idle computational capacity of consumer smartphones to enable distributed task processing at scale; however, widespread real-world adoption remains constrained by the absence of developer-oriented frameworks capable of transparently managing device heterogeneity, fault tolerance, and connectivity volatility. This paper introduces CROWDio, a centralized MCdC platform comprising three tightly integrated subsystems: (i) a declarative SDK that abstracts distributed execution to a single function annotation, eliminating the need for explicit parallelism management; (ii) a tiered checkpointing mechanism that enables fault-tolerant task resumption under the memory and execution constraints inherent to mobile runtimes; and (iii) a pluggable multi-criteria scheduling framework driven by continuous live device telemetry, supporting interchangeable decision strategies without modification to the dispatch core. Empirical evaluation across six heterogeneous Android devices spanning CPU-bound, AI/NLP inference, and data-parallel workloads demonstrates that capability-aware adaptive scheduling reduces total execution time by up to 56.9% relative to naive round-robin dispatch, while the checkpointing subsystem incurs a bounded overhead of only 2-3 s per task regardless of checkpoint frequency. A system-wide Jain's Fairness Index of 0.889 confirms equitable and stable workload distribution across heterogeneous worker devices.

</details>


### [90] [Minimizing Intellectual Property Risks via Self-Stabilizing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19454)
*Ken Kennedy,Iman Evazzade*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用分层自稳定算法进行宏观知识产权风险评估，支持多维度风险分析，并通过同时考虑最优解和次优解的方式，实现风险的灵活最小化管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在宏观层面进行知识产权风险评估时，需要一种能够支持多维度定义并灵活应对动态环境的算法框架，以实现风险最小化的目标。

Method: 采用分层架构的自稳定算法，评估知识产权风险在多个维度上的表现，并通过算法设计同时支持最优解和次优解的探索，以平衡风险最小化与计算可行性。

Result: 通过分层自稳定机制，能够在动态环境中持续维护知识产权风险评估的有效性，同时通过引入次优解搜索策略，拓展了风险管理方案的灵活性和适应性。

Conclusion: 本研究通过分层自稳定算法框架，为宏观层面的知识产权风险管理提供了灵活的解决方案，同时兼顾了最优解和次优解的探索，扩展了风险管理策略的选择空间。

Abstract: In this paper, we examine the use of self-stabilizing algorithms, operating in a hierarchical manner, to determine intellectual property risks at a macro level. We are both interested in finding a solution that will support all defined intellectual property dimensions as well as suboptimal solutions in order to minimize risk.

</details>


### [91] [DPC: A Distributed Page Cache over CXL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19494)
*Shai Bergman,Zhe Yang,Julien Eudine,Giorgio Negro,Onur Mutlu,Arash Tavakkol,Ji Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DPC是一个构建在CXL 3.0内存语义上的操作系统级分布式页缓存系统，通过强制单副本不变式（每个文件页仅有一个所有者节点持有一份DRAM副本，其他节点通过CXL远程访问）来解决传统分布式文件系统中的数据冗余、内存利用率低和一致性协议开销过高的问题。在CXL仿真平台上的真实工作负载测试显示可实现最高12.4倍的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代分布式文件系统采用无协调的节点本地页缓存架构，导致三个核心问题：1）热点数据在集群内大量冗余复制，2）集群聚合DRAM容量未被有效利用，3）基于锁的一致性协议引入过高开销。

Method: 采用单副本不变式（Single-copy invariant）：每个文件页仅有一个持有唯一DRAM副本的所有者节点，其他节点通过CXL远程映射访问而非复制数据。实现于CXL 3.0仿真框架。

Result: 在实际工作负载评测中，DPC实现最高12.4倍加速，几何平均加速达5.6倍。

Conclusion: DPC通过单一所有权设计消除了数据冗余，通过CXL 3.0远程映射替代锁协议消除了一致性开销，为分布式文件系统提供了高效的内存级共享方案。

Abstract: Modern distributed file systems rely on uncoordinated, per node page caches that replicate hot data locally across the cluster. While ensuring fast local access, this architecture underutilizes aggregate cluster DRAM capacity through massive data redundancy and incurs prohibitive coherence overhead via heavyweight, lock-based protocols. In this paper, we focus on the design of a distributed page cache that treats the entire cluster's main memory as a single cache budget while preserving standard file-system interfaces and semantics. We present Distributed Page Cache (DPC), an OS-level, distributed page cache built on top of Compute Express Link (CXL) 3.0 memory semantics. DPC enforces a single-copy invariant at page granularity: each file page has exactly one owner node holding the sole resident DRAM copy, and other nodes access it via CXL-based remote mappings rather than creating replicas of the page. DPC is implemented end-to-end on a CXL-based emulation framework that models multi-host CXL 3.0 memory fabrics, enabling detailed evaluation in the absence of widespread hardware. Across real-world and representative data-sharing workloads, DPC delivers speedups of up to 12.4X, with a geometric-mean speedup of 5.6X.

</details>


### [92] [ReaLB: Real-Time Load Balancing for Multimodal MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19503)
*Yingping Wang,Yi Wu,Xiangyu Wu,Junwei Cui,Weilin Cai,Zhijiang Guo,Jiayi Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ReaLB是一种针对多模态MoE推理的实时负载均衡方法，通过动态调整不同EP rank上专家的计算精度来平衡负载，利用FP4低精度计算加速视觉密集型rank，同时将精度转换开销隐藏在dispatch阶段。该方法无需冗余专家即可实现1.29倍加速，精度损失小于1.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多模态MoE（MMoE）推理中，不同模态的专家负载高度动态且分布不均，特别是在大batch size的prefill阶段，视觉token主导输入序列，导致在专家并行（EP）下出现严重的负载不均衡问题，使得部分设备过载而降低整体系统吞吐量。

Method: ReaLB在运行时动态调整MoE专家的计算精度，采用逐EP-rank的策略：对于视觉负载重的rank分配低精度计算（利用FP4 Tensor Cores），同时在dispatch阶段内隐藏精度转换的开销，实现layer-wise的即时专家精度变换。

Result: 在代表性MMoE模型上，ReaLB实现了1.29倍的layer-level加速，同时将精度损失控制在1.2%以内。

Conclusion: ReaLB通过动态调整MoE专家的计算精度，在不需要冗余专家或额外内存分配的情况下，实现了显著的推理加速，同时将精度损失控制在可接受范围内。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are widely used in modern large language models and multimodal models. However, inference efficiency is often limited by highly dynamic and skewed expert workloads across different modalities. During the prefill stage with large batch sizes, vision tokens frequently dominate the input sequences. Under expert parallelism (EP), this leads to severe load imbalance, where a subset of devices becomes overloaded, reducing overall system throughput.
  We propose ReaLB, a real-time load balancing method for multimodal MoE (MMoE) inference that introduces zero scheduling overhead. ReaLB dynamically adjusts the computation precision of MoE experts at runtime on a per-EP-rank basis. For ranks dominated by vision-heavy experts, ReaLB assigns lower-precision computation to improve execution efficiency by exploiting FP4 Tensor Cores. ReaLB does not require redundant experts or additional memory allocation. Instead, it performs layer-wise expert precision transformation on the fly and hides the associated overhead within the dispatch phase before MoE computation. Experiments on representative MMoE models show that ReaLB achieves 1.29x layer-level speedup while limiting accuracy loss to within 1.2%.

</details>

### [93] [The Public Health and Environmental Surveillance Open Data Model (PHES-ODM) Version 3: An Open, Relational Data Model and Interoperability Framework for Wastewater Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18762)
*Mathew Thomson,Jean-David Therrien,Nikho Hizon,Janet Lin,Martin Wellman,Eugen-Sorin Sion,Carol Bennett,Peter Van Rolleghem,Douglas Manuel*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PHES-ODM v3，这是一款用于废水监测的开放数据模型标准，旨在解决WWS数据碎片化问题。新版本新增了公共卫生行动表、外部仓储链接、分析工作流文档等功能，支持复杂关系链接，并提供跨格式映射工具。与6种其他WWS标准在25个特征上进行了对比，已被加拿大公卫署和EU系统采纳，在25国使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 废水监测（WWS）在COVID-19大流行期间展现了重要的公共卫生监测价值，但其长期应用受限于数据系统碎片化、元数据实践不一致以及互操作性差等问题。

Method: 开发了开放协作框架，支持FAIR原则（可发现、可访问、可互操作、可重用）；v3版本新增公共卫生行动表、外部仓储链接（GISAID、GenBank）、分析工作流文档，支持复杂关系链接；提供跨格式映射工具（PHA4GE、CDC NWSS）；与其他6种WWS数据标准在25个特征维度进行对比。

Result: 已被加拿大公共卫生署和欧盟污水哨兵系统采纳，在25个国家以上使用；v3版本解决了互操作性和数据可用性的持续障碍。

Conclusion: PHES-ODM v3通过平衡健壮性与可用性，提供了可扩展的模块化基础设施，可适应多种WWS和环境监测项目。

Abstract: Wastewater surveillance (WWS) has emerged as a valuable tool for public health surveillance, particularly since the COVID-19 pandemic. Its long-term utility is constrained, however, by fragmented data systems, inconsistent metadata practices, and poor interoperability. The Public Health and Environmental Surveillance Open Data Model (PHES-ODM) was developed as an open, collaborative framework to standardize WWS data and support transparent, ethical data use aligned with FAIR principles. Adopted by the Public Health Agency of Canada and adapted by the EU Sewage Sentinel System, the model is now used in over 25 countries. This paper introduces version 3 of the model, which addresses persistent barriers to interoperability and data utility. Key enhancements include new tables for public health actions, external repository linkages (e.g., GISAID, GenBank), and analytical workflow documentation, as well as support for complex relational linkages across sites, samples, measures, and populations. Tools for mapping across other data formats, including PHA4GE and the US CDC National Wastewater Surveillance System, and for supporting long and wide data formats are also introduced. We compare PHES-ODM against six other WWS data standards across 25 features. Balancing robustness with usability, PHES-ODM v3 provides a scalable, modular infrastructure adaptable to diverse WWS and environmental surveillance programs.

</details>


### [94] [Heuristic Search Space Partitioning for Low-Latency Multi-Tenant Cloud Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19057)
*Prashant Kumar Pathak,Chandra Biksheswaran Mouleeswaran,Rama Teja Repaka*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对大规模多租户云安全平台中宽泛查询导致P95延迟超60秒的问题，本文提出HSSPS系统，通过动态谓词注入实现搜索空间逻辑分区，在不修改物理Schema的情况下将P95延迟从61秒降至2秒，吞吐量提升8-10倍。该方案无需服务端会话状态，通过客户端页面令牌维护跨分区遍历状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模多租户云安全平台中，跨账户的宽泛查询导致数据库基础设施饱和，P95延迟超过60秒。实验发现缓冲池缓存压力是主要延迟驱动因素：相同查询计划下，工作集在内存中仅需3.7秒，但被并发负载驱逐后需94秒。现有查询计划优化无法解决此问题，唯一有效干预是减少每个查询需访问的页面数。

Method: 提出HSSPS（启发式搜索空间分区系统），包含两个核心组件：1) 两阶段启发式引擎 - 选择分区键值并对候选查询计划进行评分；2) 客户端页面令牌 - 维护跨分区遍历状态，无需服务端会话，支持水平扩展。系统通过动态谓词注入实现搜索空间的逻辑分区，无需Schema修改。

Result: 受控评估显示：P95延迟降低50-97%（高基数查询达95-97%），吞吐量提升8-10倍，平均活动会话数减少41倍。生产环境部署后，P95延迟从61秒降至2秒，在14000+查询的测量窗口内保持稳定。

Conclusion: 该技术可推广至任何执行宽泛查询的大型共享数据库多租户系统，尤其适用于物理Schema修改不切实际的场景。核心贡献在于将物理存储问题转化为逻辑分区问题，通过启发式方法在查询时自动识别最优分区策略。

Abstract: Large-scale cloud security platforms must continuously query millions of structured cloud resource records distributed across thousands of tenant accounts. Broad, account-spanning queries saturate database infrastructure, producing P95 latencies exceeding 60 seconds. We identify buffer cache pressure as the dominant latency driver: in a controlled experiment, the same query executing with the same plan completed in 3.7 seconds when its working set was memory-resident and 94 seconds when concurrent load had evicted those pages. No query plan optimization can address this; the only effective intervention is reducing the number of pages each query must touch. We present the Heuristic Search Space Partitioning System (HSSPS), a query-time optimization layer that logically partitions the search space through dynamic predicate injection, without schema modification. A two-phase heuristic engine selects partition key values and scores candidate query plans before execution. A client-side page token maintains cross-partition traversal state without server-side sessions, enabling horizontal scalability. Controlled evaluation across representative query types demonstrates 50-97% P95 latency reduction (95-97% on high-cardinality queries), 8-10x throughput improvement, and 41x reduction in average active sessions. Production rollout across live multi-tenant traffic reduced P95 latency from 61s to 2s across successive releases, sustained over 14,000 eligible queries per measurement window. The technique generalizes to any multi-tenant system where broad queries execute against large shared databases and physical schema modification is impractical.

</details>


### [95] [Demonstrating Online Schema Alignment in Decentralized Knowledge Graphs Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19205)
*Bryan-Elliott Tam,Pieter Colpaert,Ruben Taelman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Decentralized Knowledge Graphs querying enables integrating distributed data without centralization, but is highly sensitive to vocabulary heterogeneity. Query issuers cannot realistically anticipate all vocabulary mismatches, especially when alignment rules are local, scoped, or discovered at runtime. We present an online schema alignment approach for Link Traversal Query Processing (LTQP) that discovers, scopes, and applies alignment rules dynamically during query execution while preserving traversal behavior. This demo paper demonstrates the approach on a decentralized social-media scenario through a web interface built on a Comunica-based LTQP engine. Source code, a CLI, and a reusable library are publicly available. The demonstration shows that online schema alignment recovers complete query results with low overhead, providing a practical foundation for web-scale reasoning in LTQP systems.

</details>
